## 2. pstatus / pcheck / gate verdict references
pcheck is a function
pcheck () 
{ 
    set -euo pipefail;
    _mme_require_observer;
    "$MME_VENV_PY" "$MME_OBSERVER" --once --stream-count 2 --error-count 10 --raw-json-chars 500
}

## 9. Recommended patch order
1. Resolve controlled-paper config conflict into one fail-closed source of truth.
2. Add/repair pstatus runtime verdict path if missing.
3. Freeze or explicitly approve current dirty repo patch stack.
4. Fix/waive expected missing files with documented reason.
